For a variable h_k:
- if k>=d, it appears as h_k in first factor: derivative 1 (from its linear) - h_{k-d} (from -h_k h_{k-d}).
- if k+d <= n-1 (k<n-d), it appears as h_{(k+d)-d}=h_k in second factor: term h_{k+d}(-h_k), derivative -h_{k+d}.
So total for k: [if k>=d: 1 - h_{k-d}] + [if k<n-d: -h_{k+d}], times dx*w. This differs from my earlier for pos/neg. For pos d: c_pos=∑_{i=0}^{n-1-d} h_i(1-h_{i+d}). For h_k:
- if k<n-d source: 1 - h_{k+d}

grad_pair = dx[ w_pos[d]*( (to h_i: 1-h_j) + (to h_j: -h_i) ) + w_neg[d]*( (to h_j: 1-h_i) + (to h_i: -h_j) ) ].

We can compute gradient efficiently via Python loops over d accumulating to arrays. For n=250 w length 499, loops with vector operations per d. E.g.:
grad = np.zeros(n)
for d in range(1,n):
  wp=weights[n-1+d]; wn=weights[n-1-d]
  if wp: 
    i=0:n-d; j=d:n
    grad[i] += dx*wp*(1 - h[j])
    grad[j] += dx*wp*(-h[i])
  if wn:
    i=0:n-d; j=d:n
    # h_j source: grad[j] += dx*wn*(1 - h[i])
    # h_i target: grad[i] += dx*wn*(-h[j])
    grad[j] += dx*wn*(1 - h[i])
    grad[i] += dx*wn*(-h[j])
For lag0: grad += dx*w0*(1-2h)
This handles both signs. Need check constant term signs: For pos pair weight wp, derivative wrt h_i 1-h_j, wrt h_j -h_i. If h_i=1,h_j=0, derivative wrt h_i -? increasing h_i increases overlap, derivative 1, correct; increasing h_j decreases, derivative -1, correct. Good.
This vector loop O(n^2) but with slices, for n=250 ~31k operations, actually 2 slices per d length decreasing; 249*~250 ~62k vector additions per gradient. Very fast. For n=1000 ~500k, ok. So analytic gradient cheap.

Softmax weights w require computing c = correlate* dx; m=max; exp=exp(alpha*(c-m)); w=exp/sum. For large α, many zeros. Compute stable. Use `np.exp` maybe underflow ok. Coordinate descent algorithm:
- Maintain feasible h, C raw exact.
- Maintain current max M.
- Compute active weights w (e.g., exact active or softmax) and gradient g = ∑ w ∂c/∂h.
- A feasible transfer from j to i (i += δ, j -= δ) has directional derivative at δ=0: g_j? For smooth objective, derivative δ = g_i - g_j? Need sign: objective L derivative dot d where d_i=1,d_j=-1: g_i - g_j. If negative, δ positive decreases L. So choose pairs with g_i < g_j for positive transfer? Wait if g_i - g_j <0, increasing i decreasing j decreases L. So transfer from high g_j to low g_i.
- We can find best pair by scanning i,j? O(n^2) just for gradient difference, okay 31k per iteration. For each pair, approximate directional decrease ~ (g_j-g_i)*δ but bounds. Could select top pairs. Then perform exact line search on f(δ) over interval. This is like coordinate descent with active smooth gradient and exact line search for max. Could be much faster and may escape local better than SLSQP.

But line search over feasible interval for each selected pair. If we perform O(100) pairs per sweep, each 50 evals O(n) ~62500 ops, fine. Sweeps many. We can update C exactly via vector operations. We can stop a sweep if no improvement.

Could also update multiple coordinates? Pair coordinate descent max of convex in δ for fixed pair; suboptimal but effective.

However, exact C after move: C += δ V + δ^2 W. V = Δ_i - Δ_j. We can precompute Δ_i current? But after each accepted move, h changes, so Δ vectors for all coordinates change linearly. But if we do many pair evaluations, constructing zeros length m each time may cost. We can maybe compute V directly as slices:
V = (ones_minus_h - ones_minus_h?) source_i - source_j: source index ranges shifted by i-j; target_i - target_j. We could create V zeros. Simpler compute delta_coord function with preallocated array?   if hi-lo < 1e-15: return None
  invphi = 0.61803398875
  x1 = lo + invphi*(hi-lo); x2=hi - invphi*(hi-lo) # x1<x2? Actually x1=0.382, x2=0.618.
  y1=eval(x1); y2=eval(x2)
  for _ in range(30):
    if hi-lo < 1e-12*max(1,hi-lo): break
    if y1 < y2:
       hi=x2; x2=x1; y2=y1; x1=lo+invphi*(hi-lo); y1=eval(x1)

```
def eval_pair_delta(C,V,r1,r2,delta):
   base = C + delta*V
   mb = base.max()
   q = delta*delta
   if r1>=0:
      val = base[r1]+q
      if val>mb: mb=val
   if r2>=0 and r2!=r1:
      val=base[r2]+q; ...
   return mb
```

Pseudo:
```
def weighted_gradient(h, w, n):
    m = 2*n-1
    g = np.zeros(n)
    w0 = w[n-1]
    if w0 != 0:
        g += w0 * (1.0 - 2.0*h)
    for d in range(1,n):
        wp = w[n-1+d]
        wn = w[n-1-d]
        a = slice(0, n-d)
        b = slice(d, n)
        if wp != 0.0:
            g[a] += wp * (1.0 - h[b])
            g[b] -= wp * h[a]
        if wn != 0.0:
            g[b] += wn * (1.0 - h[a])
            g[a] -= wn * h[b]
    return g
```
Check signs for wneg: c_neg pairs i source? wneg term ∑_{i=d}^{n-1} h_i(1-h_{i-d}). For pair a (source? i-d) and b(i): pairs x=a,y=b. Orientation - weight: source b, target a: derivative wrt b (source) 1 - h_a; wrt a target -h_b. So g[b] += wn*(1-h[a]); g[a] -= wn*h[b]. Correct.

Candidate top list: To avoid O(n^2) Python if n larger (500): maybe vectorized. For each low i and high j. We'll also random.
```
def make_candidates(h,g,n, rng, max_cand, frac_lo=0.08):
   L = max(4, int(n*frac_lo))
   order = np.argsort(g)
   low = set(order[:L]); high = set(order[-L:])
   cand=[]
   for i in order[:L]:
      if h[i] >= 1.0: continue
      for j in order[-L:]:
         if i==j: continue
         if h[j] <= 0.0: continue
         hi = min(1.0-h[i], h[j])
         if hi > 1e-14:
            score = g[j]-g[i]
            if score > 0:
               cand.append((score,i,j))
   # random
   nrand = min(100, max_cand)
   for _ in range(nrand):
      i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n)); if i==j continue
      if g[i] < g[j]: pos=(i,j), score=g[j]-g[i]
      else: pos=(j,i), score=g[i]-g[j]
      hi=...
      if hi>1e-14 and score>0: cand.append...
   cand.sort reverse by score
   # unique pairs (unordered) maybe use key i<j? Since we always low i high j, no duplicates? random may duplicate.
   unique {}
   limit max_cand
```
Maybe also include local neighbors? Pair transfer far may be captured. But gradient extremes maybe too sparse. Could include random. Good.

Line search function with r1/r2. Need V compute:
```
def pair_update_vector(h,i,j,n):
   V=np.zeros(2*n-1)
   # source i: indices n-1-i : 2*n-1-i add 1-h
   V[n-1-i : 2*n-1-i] += (1.0-h)
   # source j: subtract
   V[n-1-j : 2*n-1-j] -= (1.0-h)
   # target i: indices i : n+i add -h[::-1]
   V[i : n+i] += -h[::-1]
   # target j subtract target? Since V=Δ_i-Δ_j; Δ_j target: indices j:j+n values -h[::-1]; subtract => +h[::-1]
   V[j : n+j] += h[::-1]
   return V
```
Check source slices: For k=i: start n-1-i, length n, end 2n-1-i exclusive. For i=n-1, end 2n-1-(n-1)=n, start0 length n indices0..n-1? Wait for k=n-1, source t=0 idx n-1-(n-1)=0, t=n-1 idx 2n-2-(n-1)=n-1. yes. For i=0, start n-1, end 2n-1 indices n-1..2n-2. Good.
Target slice for k=i: indices i:i+n values -h[::-1]. For i=0: indices0..n-1 values -h[::-1] (t=n-1 to0), correct. For i=n-1: indices n-1..2n-2 values -h[::-1], target t=n-1? For k=n-1, target idx n-1+(n-1)-t =2n-2-t; t=0 idx2n-2 first value -h0; t=n-1 idx n-1 last value -h_{n-1}. h[::-1] length n: first -h0 at idx n-1, last -h_{n-1} at idx2n-2? Wait slice ascending idx n-1..2n-2 values h[::-1]=h_{n-1}...h0. But expected idx n-1 corresponds t=n-1 value -h_{n-1}; idx2n-2 t=0 -h0. So values should h[::-1] (last? h_{n-1},...,h0) yes. Good. For i=0 expected idx0 t=1 -h1, idx1 t=0 -h0; h[::-1] for n=2 = [h1,h0] with minus, yes. Good.
Then V = source_i - source_j + target_i - target_j? Wait Δ_i = source_i + target_i (target values -h[::-1]). Δ_j = source_j + target_j. So V=Δ_i-Δ_j = source_i - source_j + target_i - target_j. But in code above I did `V[i:i+n] += -h[::-1]` for target_i and `V[j:n+j] += h[::-1]` for -target_j. Correct (because -(-h[::-1])=+h[::-1]). Source_j subtracted. Good.

`eval_pair_delta` top-level:
```
def eval_pair_delta(C,V,r1,r2,delta):
    if delta == 0: return C.max() # but no need
    base = C + delta*V
    mb = float(base.max())
    q = delta*delta
    v = base[r1] + q
    if v > mb: mb = v
    if r2 != r1:
       v = base[r2]+q; if v>mb: mb=v
    return mb
```
Potential memory/time: In golden, base reallocated. Could pass a preallocated tmp? We'll make top-level `eval_pair_delta_pre(C,V,r1,r2,delta,tmp)` where tmp filled. But no closures. In line_search, we can allocate tmp = np.empty_like(C) outside loop. But `np.empty_like` inside line_search ok. To avoid nested, implement line_search with manual repeat. However eval function called many times; if it allocates each call, overhead but acceptable maybe. Let's estimate: coordinate sweeps 200 pairs *30 evals =6000 allocations of length500 => 3M floats, okay. For n=500 length1000, 6M, okay. But if many sweeps, maybe 100000 allocations, overhead. Could optimize.

def eval_pair_delta_into(C,V,r1,r2,delta,tmp):
    tmp[:] = C
    if delta != 0: tmp += delta*V  # this creates temp? `tmp += delta*V` creates product. Could use `np.multiply(V, delta, out=tmp2)`? No.

Line search function top-level:
```
def pair_line_search(C,V,r1,r2,lo,hi,current_M, maxit=30, improve_tol=1e-13):
  if hi <= lo + 1e-16 or hi-lo < 1e-14: return None
  c1 = lo + 0.381966*(hi-lo); c2 = hi - 0.381966*(hi-lo)
  y1 = eval_pair_delta(C,V,r1,r2,c1)
  y2 = eval_pair_delta(C,V,r1,r2,c2)
  for _ in range(maxit):
     if hi-lo <= 1e-13*max(1.0, hi-lo): break
     if y1 < y2:
        hi = c2; c2=c1; y2=y1; c1 = lo + 0.381966*(hi-lo); y1=eval(...)
     else:
        lo = c1; c1=c2; y1=y2; c2 = hi - 0.381966*(hi-lo); y2=eval(...)
  # evaluate endpoints and c1/c2 choose best
  candidates = [lo,hi,c1,c2]
  best_y=current_M; best_d=0
  for d in cand: if eval < best_y: ...
  if best_y < current_M - improve_tol: return best_d,best_y
  return None
```

```
def smooth_obj_jac(x,n,alpha):
   C = np.correlate(x,1-x,mode='full')
   dx=2.0/n
   c=C*dx; m=c.max()
   shifted=alpha*(c-m)
   e=np.exp(np.clip(shifted,-700,0))
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0: e[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; s=1.0
   w=e/s
   value=m + np.log(s)/alpha
   g=dx*weighted_gradient(x,w,n)
   return float(value), g
```

def optimize_grid(h,n,deadline,rng,do_slsqp=True):
  h = project_feasible(h.copy(),n)
  C = compute_C_raw(h,n); M=max(C); best_c=dx*M
  best_h=h.copy()
  # name variables not closure
  # 1. coordinate phase 1 with time limit maybe 25% of remaining or max loops
  remaining = deadline - time.time()
  if remaining > 20:
     phase_deadline = time.time() + min(remaining*0.35, 120) # or if n<=300 180
     h,C,M,best_c = coord_search(h,C,phase_deadline,rng, max_sweeps=500, topk=120, evals=35)
  # 2. SLSQP phase
  if do_slsqp and time.time() < deadline - 30:
     alphas = [100.0, 1000.0, 5000.0, 20000.0]
     # start with current best_h? Use h, recompute C
     for alpha in alphas:
        if time.time() > deadline - 20: break
        # choose maxiter based on remaining and n
        rem = deadline - time.time() - 10
        if rem < 15: break
        maxiter = 80 if n>=350 else (150 if n>=200 else 250)
        # maybe scale by rem
        if n > 500: do one alpha small
        ht = slsqp_smooth(h,best_h,n,alpha,maxiter) # returns projected maybe
        c = compute_c5(ht,n)
        if c < best_c - 1e-13:
            best_c=c; best_h=ht.copy(); h=project_feasible(ht,n)
        # if not improve, keep h? SLSQP may find same; maybe use if h improved smooth? skip
     # After SLSQP, if h not best? We optimized h; if SLSQP improved, h=best.
  # 3. final coordinate
  if time.time() < deadline - 8:
     C=M? recompute from best_h
     h,C,M,best_c = coord_search(best_h,C,time.time()+min(remaining, deadline? but careful),rng, ...)
  return best_h,best_c
```

```
def make_candidates(h,g,n,rng,topk):
   cand=[]; seen=set()
   L=max(4,min(n//4,30))
   order=np.argsort(g)
   low=order[:L]; high=order[-L:][::-1]
   for i in low:
      if h[i] >= 1.0-1e-14: continue
      for j in high:
         if i==j or h[j] <= 1e-14: continue
         key=(i,j)? Since i low g, j high g; if same unordered, could appear with swapped g? We use low/high only one direction (i low, j high). If low list and high list overlap, i may be high? But g_i<g_j by construction? Not necessarily if overlap; check score>0. key=(i,j).
         score=g[j]-g[i]
         if score>1e-10:
            hi = min(1-h[i],h[j])
            if hi>1e-14:
               if key not in seen: add
   # random pairs
   nrand = min(topk, 200)
   for _ in range(nrand):
      i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
      if i==j: continue
      if g[i] < g[j]: ii,jj=i,j; score=g[j]-g[i]
      else: ii,jj=j,i; score=g[i]-g[j]
      if score <= 1e-10: continue
      hi=min(1-h[ii],h[jj]); if hi<=1e-14 continue
      key=(ii,jj)
      if key not in seen: add
   # sort by score desc
   cand.sort(key=lambda x:x[0], reverse=True) # Lambda! Not allowed? "no closures or lambdas". Avoid lambda.
```

```

Pair update V = Δ_i - Δ_j with actual Δ. We can construct V using slices:
Δ_i source: indices i : n+i add y[::-1]
Δ_i target: indices n-1-i : 2n-1-i add -h
Δ_j similarly subtract.
So V:
```
V = zeros
V[i : n+i] += y[::-1]
V[n-1-i : 2*n-1-i] += -h
V[j : n+j] -= y[::-1]
V[n-1-j : 2*n-1-j] -= -h  # => += h
```
Let's test n=2 i=0,j=1:
y=[y0,y1]; yrev=[y1,y0]
V initial zeros 3

```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    target = n/2.0
    h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    for _ in range(200):
        s = h.sum()
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14: break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot < 1e-15: break
            h += (diff / tot) * room
        else:
            tot = s
            if tot < 1e-15: break
            h += (diff / tot) * h
        h = np.clip(h,0,1)
    # adjust tiny residual
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 0:
       if diff > 0:
          idx = np.where(h < 1.0)[0]
       else: idx=np.where(h>0)[0]
       if len(idx): h[idx] += diff/len(idx)
    return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
But if final adjustment pushes bounds, loop? Could set small. Better keep iterative. Since target integer/half, projector will converge.
Potential issue with scaling for negative diff: adding diff/tot*h may make some negative? h nonnegative, diff negative, subtract proportionally; if any h is small, could go negative but clip. Reiterate. OK.
Could use bisection projection for exact and stable. But parent works. We'll use more iterations 1000? 500 enough.

Now optimize_grid phases. Let's write top-level:
```
def coord_search(h, C, deadline, rng, alphas=(100.0,1000.0,5000.0), topk=120, maxit_line=32, max_sweeps=10000):
    n=len(h); dx=2.0/n
    h = h.copy()
    C = np.asarray(C,dtype=float) # maybe copy
    M = float(C.max())
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_M = M
    sweep=0
    for alpha in alphas:
        no_improve = 0
        while time.time() < deadline - 2:
            # recompute C from h every phase? To clean drift and M; do each sweep
            C = compute_C_raw(h,n)
            M = float(C.max())
            if M < best_M:
                best_M=M; best_h=h.copy()
            if no_improve >= 4: break
            w = weights_alpha(C,dx,alpha)
            g = weighted_gradient(h,w,n)
            cand = make_candidates(h,g,n,rng,topk)
            accepted=0
            for score,i,j in cand:
                if time.time() > deadline - 2: break
                hi = min(1.0 - h[i], h[j])
                lo = max(-h[i], h[j] - 1.0)
                # margins
                if hi <= lo: continue
                lo = lo + 1e-12
                hi = hi - 1e-12
                if hi <= lo: continue
                V = pair_update_vector(h,i,j,n)
                d = abs(i-j)
                r1 = n-1-d; r2=n-1+d
                res = pair_line_search(C,V,r1,r2,lo,hi,M,evals=maxit_line)
                if res is not None:
                    delta,newM=res
                    # clamp
                    if delta < lo: delta=lo
                    if delta > hi: delta=hi
                    q=delta*delta
                    C += delta*V
                    C[r1] += q
                    if r2 != r1: C[r2] += q
                    h[i] += delta; h[j] -= delta
                    M = newM
                    accepted += 1
                    if M < best_M:
                        best_M = M; best_h = h.copy()
            if accepted == 0:
                no_improve += 1
            else:
                no_improve = 0
            sweep +=1
            if sweep > max_sweeps: break
    # final project? maybe don't; but clip tiny
    h = np.clip(best_h? or h?)
```

Implementation:
```
def pair_line_search(C,V,r1,r2,lo,hi,current_M,maxit=32,tol=1e-10):
    span=hi-lo
    if span <= 1e-14: return None
    best_delta = 0.0
    best_y = current_M
    # quick evaluations maybe at 0? no
    c1 = lo + (1.0 - 0.61803398875)*span
    c2 = hi - (1.0 - 0.61803398875)*span
    y1 = eval_pair_delta(C,V,r1,r2,c1)
    if y1 < best_y - tol: best_y=y1; best_delta=c1
    y2 = eval_pair_delta(C,V,r1,r2,c2)
    if y2 < best_y - tol: best_y=y2; best_delta=c2
    for _ in range(maxit):
       span=hi-lo
       if span <= 1e-12*max(1.0, abs(lo), abs(hi)): break
       if y1 < y2:
          hi = c2; c2=c1; y2=y1
          c1 = lo + (1.0 - 0.61803398875)*(hi-lo)
          y1 = eval...
          if y1 < best_y - tol: ...
       else:
          lo = c1; c1=c2; y1=y2
          c2 = hi - (1.0 - 0.61803398875)*(hi-lo)
          y2=eval...
          if y2 < best_y - tol: ...
    # evaluate endpoints maybe lo,hi
    for dd in (lo,hi): ...
    if best_y < current_M - tol: return (best_delta, best_y)
    return None
```
Need if y1==y2, else branch moves lo; okay. Could stuck; but fine. Use `tol` for update best but line search continues regardless.
`eval_pair_delta` with delta=0 returns C.max() current_M; but not called at 0.

If f min at endpoint hi, f decreasing then increasing; moves lo right. Good.

Maxiter heuristic:
```
def slsqp_smooth(h,n,alpha,maxiter):
    if n > 800: maxiter=30
    if maxiter > 500: maxiter=500
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0) for _ in range(n)]
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_equal_fun,'jac':sum_equal_jac,'args':(n,)}]
    try:
      res=minimize(smooth_obj_jac, h, args=(n,alpha), method='SLSQP', jac=True, bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-14})
      return project_feasible(res.x,n)
    except Exception:
      return project_feasible(h,n)
```
`bounds` list comprehension is okay? It's not closure. Could be memory. Use `bounds = [(0.0,1.0)] * n` (same tuples immutable). Good.
SLSQP may be slow due constraints and large n; maxiter small.

`optimize_grid` with coordinate/SLSQP. Need not use nested. Let's design carefully with time.
```
def optimize_grid(h,n,deadline,rng):
    h = np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    h = project_feasible(h,n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    C = compute_C_raw(h,n)
    best_c = float(C.max())* (2.0/n)
    # coordinate phase 1: allocate up to 40% remaining or 120s? But if called multiple times, deadline absolute.
    # We'll pass max coordinate time fraction maybe.
    remaining = deadline - time.time()
    if remaining > 15:
        coord_deadline = time.time() + min(max(10.0, remaining*0.35), 180.0)
        if coord_deadline > deadline - 10: coord_deadline = deadline - 10
        h2,c2 = coord_search(h,best_c? same, coord_deadline, rng, ...)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c=c2; best_h=h2.copy(); h=h2.copy()
        else:
            # maybe coord returned best_h with same; use?
            h = project_feasible(h2,n)?
    # SLSQP if time >40 and n<=450
    if time.time() < deadline - 35 and n <= 450:
       # current h maybe best; use best_h as start
       alphas = [100.0, 1000.0]
       if deadline-time > 90 and n<=300: alphas.append(5000.0)
       for alpha in alphas:
          if time.time() > deadline - 15: break
          # maxiter based on time and n
          rem = deadline - time.time() - 5
          if rem < 10: break
          if n <= 150: mi=250
          elif n <= 300: mi=150
          else: mi=80
          # if rem large maybe double
          ht = slsqp_smooth(best_h,n,alpha,mi)
          c = compute_c5(ht,n)
          if c < best_c - 1e-13:
             best_c=c; best_h=ht.copy()
       # after SLSQP update h
       h = best_h.copy()
       # coordinate phase 2 with remaining
       if time.time() < deadline - 8:
          coord_deadline = deadline - 5
          h2,c2 = coord_search(h, compute_C_raw(h,n), coord_deadline, rng, alphas=[2000.0,10000.0], topk maybe smaller, maxit 30)
          if c2 < best_c:
             best_c=c2; best_h=h2.copy()
    else:
       if time.time() < deadline - 8:
          coord_deadline = deadline - 5
          h2,c2 = coord_search(h, compute_C_raw(h,n), coord_deadline, rng, ...)
          update
    return best_h, best_c
```

Need `eval_pair_delta` top-level uses `np.max`. It allocates base. Could optimize if delta=0.
```
def eval_pair_delta(C, V, r1, r2, delta):
    if delta == 0.0:
        return float(C.max())
    base = C + delta * V
    mb = float(base.max())
    q = delta * delta
    v1 = base[r1] + q
    if v1 > mb: mb = v1
    v2 = base[r2] + q
    if v2 > mb: mb = v2
    return mb
```
If r1/r2 maybe same? distance 0 not; but safe.
This allocates product `delta*V`; if delta=0 skip. Could use `np.dot`? No.

def pair_line_search(C, V, r1, r2, lo, hi, current_M, maxit=32, tol=1e-10):
    span = hi - lo
    if span <= 1e-14:
        return None
    best_d = 0.0
    best_y = current_M
    if not np.isfinite(current_M): ...

    c2 = hi - frac*span
    y1 = eval_pair_delta(...)
    if y1 < best_y - tol: ...
    y2 = ...
    for it in range(maxit):
       if hi - lo <= 1e-12 * max(1.0, abs(lo), abs(hi)):
          break

          hi = c2
          c2 = c1; y2 = y1
          c1 = lo + frac*(hi-lo)
          y1 = eval...
          if y1 < best_y - tol: ...
       else:
          lo = c1
          c1 = c2; y1 = y2
          c2 = hi - frac*(hi-lo)
          y2 = eval...

    # endpoints
    for dd in (lo, hi):
       y=eval...
       if y < best_y - tol: ...
    if best_y < current_M - tol:
       return (best_d, best_y)
    return None
```

`pair_update_vector`:
```
def pair_update_vector(h, i, j, n):
    m = 2*n - 1
    V = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
    yrev = (1.0 - h)[::-1]
    neg_h = -h
    V[i : n + i] += yrev
    V[n - 1 - i : 2*n - 1 - i] += neg_h
    V[j : n + j] -= yrev
    V[n - 1 - j : 2*n - 1 - j] = V[n - 1 - j : 2*n - 1 - j] + h  # or += h
    return V
```
Be careful `neg_h` not modify. `+=` with view okay.
Could yrev allocate copy; fine. For speed, `add_y = 1.0 - h; add_yr = add_y[::-1]` and `neg_h = -h`. It creates multiple arrays per candidate. OK.

`make_candidates`:
```
def make_candidates(h, g, n, rng, topk):
    cand = []
    seen = set()
    L = max(4, min(30, n // 8))
    order = np.argsort(g)
    low = order[:L]
    high = order[-L:][::-1]
    for ii in low:
        i = int(ii)
        if h[i] >= 1.0 - 1e-14:
            continue
        for jj in high:
            j = int(jj)
            if i == j:
                continue
            hi = min(1.0 - h[i], h[j])
            if hi <= 1e-14:
                continue
            score = float(g[j] - g[i])
            if score <= 1e-9:
                continue
            key = min(i,j)*n + max(i,j)
            if key not in seen:
                # ensure stored direction maybe low g to high g? We can keep i,j if score>0 else swap
                if g[i] > g[j]:
                    i,j = j,i
                    score = -score
                # but if after swap hi? recompute?
                hi2 = min(1-h[i],h[j])
                if hi2 > 1e-14:
                    seen.add(key)
                    cand.append((score, i, j))
    # random
    nrand = min(max(topk, 30), 300)
    for _ in range(nrand):
        i = int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
        if i==j: continue
        if g[i] < g[j]: i2,j2=i,j; score=float(g[j]-g[i])
        else: i2,j2=j,i; score=float(g[i]-g[j])
        if score <= 1e-9: continue
        hi = min(1-h[i2],h[j2])
        if hi <= 1e-14: continue
        key = min(i2,j2)*n + max(i2,j2)
        if key not in seen:
            seen.add(key); cand.append((score,i2,j2))
    if len(cand) > topk:
        # Need top topk by score without lambda: sort then cut
        cand.sort(reverse=True)
        cand = cand[:topk]
    else:
        cand.sort(reverse=True)
    return cand
```
Set top-level `CAND_SCORE_TOL = 1e-9` maybe. Score scale? g values could be O(n) ~250, differences. 1e-9 very small. OK.
Potential if g has NaN? no.

Coordinates full interval: score orientation low to high, but line search full covers both. If score small but exact curvature might improve; random maybe.

`coord_search` details:
```
def coord_search(h, deadline, rng, alphas=None, topk=120, line_evals=32, max_no_improve=4):
    n = len(h)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = project_feasible(h,n)
    C = compute_C_raw(h,n)
    M = float(C.max())
    best_h = h.copy(); best_M = M
    if alphas is None:
        if n <= 200: alphas=(100.0,1000.0,5000.0)
        elif n <= 400: alphas=(100.0,1000.0)
        else: alphas=(100.0,500.0)
    if n > 500:
        topk = min(topk, n//5)
        line_evals = min(line_evals, 28)
    tol_base = 1e-10 * max(1.0, M)
    sweep = 0
    for alpha in alphas:
        no_imp = 0
        while time.time() < deadline - 1.0:
            C = compute_C_raw(h,n)
            M = float(C.max())
            if M < best_M - 1e-15:
                best_M = M; best_h = h.copy()
            if no_imp >= max_no_improve:
                break
            w = weights_alpha(C,dx,alpha)
            g = weighted_gradient(h,w,n)
            cand = make_candidates(h,g,n,rng,topk)
            accepted = 0
            for item in cand:
                if time.time() > deadline - 0.5:
                    break
                score, i, j = item
                hi = min(1.0 - h[i], h[j])
                lo = max(-h[i], h[j] - 1.0)
                if hi <= lo:
                    continue
                lo += 1e-12
                hi -= 1e-12
                if hi <= lo:
                    continue
                V = pair_update_vector(h,i,j,n)
                d = abs(i-j)
                r1 = n - 1 - d
                r2 = n - 1 + d
                res = pair_line_search(C, V, r1, r2, lo, hi, M, line_evals, max(1e-12, 1e-10*M))
                if res is not None:
                    delta, newM = res
                    if delta <= lo: delta=lo
                    if delta >= hi: delta=hi
                    q = delta*delta
                    C += delta * V
                    C[r1] += q
                    if r2 != r1:
                        C[r2] += q
                    h[i] += delta
                    h[j] -= delta
                    M = newM
                    accepted += 1
                    if M < best_M:
                        best_M = M
                        best_h = h.copy()
            if accepted == 0:
                no_imp += 1
            else:
                no_imp = 0
            sweep += 1
            if sweep > 100000:
                break
    h = project_feasible(best_h,n)
    c = compute_c5(h,n)
    # If projection increased, maybe return original best_h? Better return projected h with computed c. Since constraints matter.
    return h, c
```
Note: After recompute C at start of sweep, if M > best_M due previous h not projected? But we only update h accepting improvements; M should nonincrease. However recomputing from h may yield slightly higher due rounding; best_h tracks lower. At phase end, we project best_h and compute c; projection may increase. Could instead return unprojected best_h if feasible enough. Final run must project. We can ensure bounds within margin; sum drift maybe small. `project_feasible` likely negligible. OK.
`tol_base` unused. In line search tol uses M. Good.
After accepted, `C` updated exact but h maybe not exactly due rounding; next candidate `V` uses updated h. If h drifts, C may drift from h; but we recompute at start of next sweep only. Within sweep, C exact relative to updates from original h, but after multiple updates, C is exact for cumulative if linear+quadratic updates sequentially? Yes, because each update formula exact from current h? We compute V from current h before update; after update C exact for new h. Good.
If h after update very close bounds, subsequent hi compute maybe negative due tiny; margins. Fine.

Potential issue: In `pair_line_search`, if the current C vector has M, but due previous updates C maybe has max at the two q indices etc. Good.

`weights_alpha` with `alpha` objective. Use C raw and dx:
```
def weights_alpha(C, dx, alpha):
    c = C * dx
    m = float(c.max())
    shifted = alpha * (c - m)
    # if alpha==0: uniform
    e = np.exp(np.clip(shifted, -740.0, 0.0))
    s = e.sum()
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
         w=np.zeros_like(C); w[int(C.argmax())]=1.0; return w
    return e / s
```
`c` allocates length m. Could avoid by shifted=alpha*dx*(C-m_raw). Use raw m = C.max(); shifted=alpha*dx*(C-m_raw). This is same. Better:
```
m_raw = float(C.max())
shifted = alpha * dx * (C - m_raw)
```
No c array. Good.
If alpha*dx small? OK.
`alpha` objective; dx~0.008, alpha=100 => raw 0.8. C raw gaps maybe objective gap 0.01 -> raw0.01/dx? Wait C raw gap = obj_gap/dx. α_obj*dx*(C-M)=α_obj*(obj-M). Good. So compute `shifted = alpha * dx * (C - m_raw)`.

`smooth_obj_jac`: c_obj = C*dx. Use m_obj = max(C)*dx. shifted=alpha*(C*dx - m_obj). Equivalent. L=m_obj+... value. grad=dx*weighted. We can compute `shifted = alpha*dx*(C - m_raw)`. `Lobj = m_raw*dx + log(s)/alpha`. Good.
```
def smooth_obj_jac(x,n,alpha):
   x=np.clip(x,0,1)
   C=np.correlate(x,1-x,mode='full')
   dx=2.0/n
   m_raw=float(C.max())
   shifted=alpha*dx*(C-m_raw)
   e=np.exp(np.clip(shifted,-740,0))
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0: ...
   w=e/s
   value=m_raw*dx + np.log(s)/alpha
   g=dx*weighted_gradient(x,w,n)
   return float(value), g
```
If alpha==0? Could return exact max, grad active? But SLSQP uses positive. If alpha very small >0. We'll not pass 0.
SLSQP `ftol=1e-14` maybe too strict causing many iter; with maxiter small. Use `ftol=1e-12`.

`slsqp_smooth`:
```
def slsqp_smooth(h,n,alpha,maxiter):
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0) for _ in range(n)]
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_equal_fun,'jac':sum_equal_jac,'args':(n,)}]
    try:
       res=minimize(smooth_obj_jac, h, args=(n,float(alpha)), method='SLSQP', jac=True, bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':int(maxiter),'ftol':1e-12})
       return project_feasible(res.x,n)
    except Exception:
       return project_feasible(h,n)
```
Potential memory/time for large n bounds list. If n>600 skip.

`optimize_grid` more refined:
```
def optimize_grid(h, n, deadline, rng, do_slsqp=True):
    h = project_feasible(h.copy(),n)
    best_h = h.copy(); best_c = compute_c5(best_h,n)
    # initial coordinate
    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if rem > 20:
       # allocate fraction
       if rem > 200:
           t1 = time.time() + min(120.0, rem*0.35)
       else:
           t1 = time.time() + rem*0.55
       if t1 > deadline - 5: t1 = deadline - 5
       h2,c2 = coord_search(h,t1,rng)
       if c2 < best_c: best_c=c2; best_h=h2
       else: best_h = h2? If c2 not lower but maybe due projection increased; keep best_h.
    # symmetrical transform
    if time.time() < deadline - 10:
       hs = 0.5*(best_h + best_h[::-1]); hs=project_feasible(hs,n); cs=compute_c5(hs,n)
       if cs < best_c: best_c=cs; best_h=hs
    # SLSQP
    if do_slsqp and n <= 450 and time.time() < deadline - 40:
       alphas = [500.0, 3000.0]
       if n <= 250 and time.time() < deadline - 70: alphas.append(10000.0)
       for alpha in alphas:
          if time.time() > deadline - 15: break
          rem = deadline - time.time()
          if rem < 12: break
          if n <= 150: mi=100
          elif n <= 300: mi=60
          else: mi=40
          ht = slsqp_smooth(best_h,n,alpha,mi)
          ct = compute_c5(ht,n)
          if ct < best_c:
             best_c=ct; best_h=ht
    # final coordinate
    if time.time() < deadline - 5:
       h2,c2 = coord_search(best_h, deadline - 2.0, rng, topk=100, line_evals=30)
       if c2 < best_c: best_c=c2; best_h=h2
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h,n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h,n)
    return best_h,best_c
```

 # final ensure

 best_c = compute_c5(best_h,best_n)
 return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)

   x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2/n

Let's define top-level functions for block:
```
def block_objective(x, h_full, idx, n, alpha):
    h2 = h_full.copy()
    h2[idx] = x
    val, g = smooth_obj_jac(h2, n, alpha)
    return val, g[idx]

def block_sum_fun(x, target):
    return float(np.sum(x) - target)

def block_sum_jac(x, target):
    return np.ones_like(x)  # or np.ones(len(x))
```

```
def block_refine(h,n,deadline,rng,alpha=1000.0,block_size=24,maxiter=120):
    best_h=project_feasible(h,n); best_c=compute_c5(best_h,n)
    # maybe 0/1 half iters
    while time.time() < deadline - 5:
       C=compute_C_raw(best_h,n); dx=2/n
       w=weights_alpha(C,dx,alpha)
       g=weighted_gradient(best_h,w,n)
       order=np.argsort(g)
       # idx gradient
       half=block_size//2
       idx_list = list(order[:half]) + list(order[-(block_size-half):][::-1])
       # unique
       idx = np.array(idx_list, dtype=int)
       # if rng and random: maybe replace half
       if rng is not None and rng.rand()<0.4:
           rand_idx = rng.choice(n, max(2, block_size//2), replace=False)
           # merge unique
           idx = np.unique(np.concatenate((idx, rand_idx)))
       if len(idx) > block_size:
           # keep first block_size? Need not break if idx unsorted? Sorted by np.unique. Could just idx=idx[:block_size]
           idx = idx[:block_size]
       if len(idx) < 2: break
       mask = np.zeros(n,dtype=bool); mask[idx]=True
       target = n/2.0 - float(np.sum(np.where(mask, 0.0, best_h))) # or best_h[~mask]
       # Ensure target feasible: between len and len, else adjust? sum fixed.
       x0 = best_h[idx].copy()
       bounds=[(0,1) for _ in range(len(idx))]
       cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':block_sum_fun,'jac':block_sum_jac,'args':(float(target),)}]
       try:
          res=minimize(block_objective, x0, args=(best_h,idx,n,alpha), method='SLSQP', jac=True, bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12})
          xnew=np.clip(res.x,0,1)
          # project block with sum target maybe
          # use small projector for block? Could just full project.
          hnew=best_h.copy(); hnew[idx]=xnew; hnew=project_feasible(hnew,n)
          c=compute_c5(hnew,n)
          if c < best_c - 1e-13:
             best_h=hnew; best_c=c
          else:
             # if no improve maybe try random? break after sAttempts?
             pass
       except Exception:
          pass
       # To avoid same gradient block no improve repeatedly, if no improve break? Could allow a few random.

def interpolate_to_n(h_old,n_old,n_new):
    if n_old == n_new:
       return project_feasible(h_old.copy(), n_new)
    dx_old=2.0/n_old; dx_new=2.0/n_new
    x_old=(np.arange(n_old)+0.5)*dx_old
    x_new=(np.arange(n_new)+0.5)*dx_new
    h_new=np.interp(x_new, x_old, h_old, left=h_old[0], right=h_old[-1])
    return project_feasible(h_new, n_new)
```

```
def project_feasible(v,n):
    target = n/2.0
    v = np.asarray(v,dtype=float)
    if target <=0: return np.zeros(n)
    if target >= n: return np.ones(n)
    lo = float(np.min(v)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(v)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(80):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       p=np.clip(v-mid,0.0,1.0)
       s=p.sum()
       if s > target:
          # need increase mid to decrease sum
          lo=mid
       else:
          hi=mid
    p=np.clip(v-(lo+hi)/2,0.0,1.0)
    # correct residual exactly
    diff=target - p.sum()
    if diff>0:
       idx=np.where(p<1.0)[0]
       if len(idx): # distribute
          # but distributing equally may exceed 1 if diff large; do iterative
          step=diff/len(idx)
          idx_order=idx
          for k in idx_order:
             add=min(step,1.0-p[k])
             p[k]+=add
             diff-=add
             if diff<=1e-15: break
       # if still diff? set maybe
    elif diff<0:
       idx=np.where(p>0.0)[0]
       step=diff/len(idx)
       for k in idx:
          sub=min(-step,p[k])
          p[k]-=sub
          diff+=sub
          if diff>=-1e-15: break
    return p
```
80 bisections each length n => for n=500, 40k ops, okay. More exact than parent's iterative. 80 iterations maybe overkill; 60 enough. We can use 50. Sum target exact enough. The residual correction handles.
However, bisection when v has NaN? no. If target near n or 0, sum monotonic; if p.sum() never reaches? Bounds lo/hi ensure at lo, v-lo = v-min(v)+1 >=1 for all? For min element: v_min - (v_min-1)=1, clipped 1; non-min >1 clip1 => sum n. At hi, v_hi - (v_max+1) <=0 for max; all <=0 => sum0. Good. target between.
This projection is Euclidean and convex; good.

Use this instead of parent projection. Note parent `project_feasible` used iterative additive to sum, not Euclidean; Euclidean may change h differently and maybe objective. But it's a valid projection; starting from h, projects onto feasible set with minimal L2. Good. It might alter active local less. Use 60 iter.
Residual correction: If diff positive and distribute to p<1, could exceed if diff large; loop until diff small. If after one pass diff remains due many at 1? Since bisection sum close, diff small. Good. For target integer, bisection error after 60 ~ range/2^60 ~1e-16; correction unnecessary. But floating sum may off.
We can simplify: after bisection return p. Need ensure sum exactly? Evaluator likely uses tolerance. Parent required exact sum? The task says equivalently sum==n/2 exactly, but floats cannot exact. It likely checks objective only after clip? It says constraints 0≤h≤1, sum(h)*dx=1. The evaluator may not enforce sum? We must be feasible. Bisection gives ~1e-14. Good.

Detailed function definitions:
```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def project_feasible(v, n):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float).copy()
    target = float(n) * 0.5
    if n == 0: return v
    if target <= 1e-15:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if target >= n - 1e-15:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    vmin = float(np.min(v)); vmax = float(np.max(v))
    lo = vmin - 1.0; hi = vmax + 1.0
    for _ in range(70):
       mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
       p = np.clip(v - mid, 0.0, 1.0)
       s = float(p.sum())
       if s > target:
           lo = mid
       else:
           hi = mid
    p = np.clip(v - 0.5*(lo+hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(p.sum())
    if diff > 1e-15:
       idx = np.where(p < 1.0)[0]
       if idx.size:
          step = diff / idx.size
          for k in idx:
             add = min(step, 1.0 - p[k])
             p[k] += add
             diff -= add
             if diff <= 1e-15: break
    elif diff < -1e-15:
       idx = np.where(p > 0.0)[0]
       if idx.size:
          step = -diff / idx.size
          for k in idx:
             sub = min(step, p[k])
             p[k] -= sub
             diff += sub
             if diff >= -1e-15: break
    return p
```
If diff remains, maybe final sum close. Could one loop not enough if step clipped for many at near1 leaving diff; but diff small. Could repeat 2 times. Add a small loop 3 times.
```
for _ in range(3):
   diff = target - p.sum()
   if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
   if diff>0: idx=...
```
Better implement loop 5.
```
    for _ in range(5):
       diff = target - float(p.sum())
       if abs(diff) < 1e-14: break
       if diff > 0: ...
```
Good.

`compute_C_raw`:
```
def compute_C_raw(h,n):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full').astype(float, copy=False)
```
`astype` maybe not needed.

`compute_c5`:
```
def compute_c5(h,n):
    C=compute_C_raw(h,n)
    return float(C.max()) * (2.0/n)
```

`weights_alpha`:
```
def weights_alpha(C, dx, alpha):
    m = float(C.max())
    shifted = float(alpha) * float(dx) * (C - m)
    e = np.exp(np.clip(shifted, -740.0, 0.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w = np.zeros_like(C, dtype=float)
        w[int(C.argmax())] = 1.0
        return w
    return e / s
```

`weighted_gradient` as above.

Potential: `weights_alpha` if C has length m and alpha huge, m_raw large; shifted product maybe `alpha*dx*(C-m)` could overflow if alpha*dx*gap huge? α up to 10000, dx~0.002, gap raw up to ~n? gap obj up to 1, shifted up to 0 negative; if C[m] max, others less, C-m≤0, no positive overflow. Good.

`smooth_obj_jac`:
```
def smooth_obj_jac(x,n,alpha):
    x = np.clip(np.asarray(x,dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    C = compute_C_raw(x,n)
    dx=2.0/n
    m_raw=float(C.max())
    shifted = float(alpha) * dx * (C - m_raw)
    e=np.exp(np.clip(shifted,-740,0))
    s=float(e.sum())
    if s<=0 or not finite:
       w=zeros; w[argmax]=1; s=1
    else: w=e/s
    value = m_raw*dx + float(np.log(s)) / float(alpha)
    g = dx * weighted_gradient(x,w,n)
    return float(value), g
```

```
if alpha <=0: value=m_raw*dx; w active? return
```

if alpha <= 0.0:
   w=zeros; w[argmax(C)]=1; return m_raw*dx, dx*weighted_gradient(x,w,n)
```

`sum_equal_fun`, `sum_equal_jac`.

`slsqp_smooth` as above. If n large, maxiter maybe 0? skip.
```
def slsqp_smooth(h,n,alpha,maxiter):
    if maxiter <=0: return project_feasible(h,n)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)] * n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_equal_fun,'jac':sum_equal_jac,'args':(n,)}]
    try:
       res = minimize(smooth_obj_jac, h, args=(n, float(alpha)), method='SLSQP', jac=True, bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'ftol':1e-12})
       return project_feasible(res.x,n)
    except Exception:
       return project_feasible(h,n)
```
If n is large 1000, bounds list 1000 okay. But SLSQP dense maybe memory. We'll call only n≤450. In run do_slsqp.

`pair_line_search` uses global.
```
def pair_line_search(C,V,r1,r2,lo,hi,current_M,maxit=32,tol=1e-10):
    span = hi - lo
    if span <= 1e-14:
       return None
    best_d = 0.0
    best_y = float(current_M)
    c1 = lo + _GOLDEN_FRAC * span
    c2 = hi - _GOLDEN_FRAC * span
    y1 = eval_pair_delta(C,V,r1,r2,c1)
    if y1 < best_y - tol: best_y=y1; best_d=c1
    y2 = eval_pair_delta(C,V,r1,r2,c2)
    if y2 < best_y - tol: best_y=y2; best_d=c2
    for _ in range(maxit):
       if hi - lo <= 1e-13 * max(1.0, abs(lo), abs(hi)):
           break
       if y1 < y2:
           hi = c2
           c2 = c1; y2 = y1
           c1 = lo + _GOLDEN_FRAC * (hi - lo)
           y1 = eval_pair_delta(C,V,r1,r2,c1)
           if y1 < best_y - tol: best_y=y1; best_d=c1
       else:
           lo = c1
           c1 = c2; y1 = y2
           c2 = hi - _GOLDEN_FRAC * (hi - lo)
           y2 = eval_pair_delta(C,V,r1,r2,c2)
           if y2 < best_y - tol: best_y=y2; best_d=c2
    # endpoints
    for dd in (lo, hi):
       y=eval_pair_delta(...)
       if y < best_y - tol: ...
    if best_y < float(current_M) - tol:
       return (float(best_d), float(best_y))
    return None
```
Potential if y1 and y2 close and min between, golden eventually. Good.
Tol default improved in coord uses dynamic. In function, tol parameter required? Give default 1e-10.

`pair_update_vector`:
```
def pair_update_vector(h,i,j,n):
    m=2*n-1
    V=np.zeros(m,dtype=float)
    yrev = (1.0 - h)[::-1]
    neg_h = -h
    V[i : n + i] += yrev
    V[n - 1 - i : 2*n - 1 - i] += neg_h
    V[j : n + j] -= yrev
    V[n - 1 - j : 2*n - 1 - j] += h
    return V
```
Check target_j should add +h (not neg). Yes.

`coord_search`: Need alphas default None. Use global time. `max_no_improve` default 4.
Inside, at end, project best_h and compute c. But if `best_h` already projected, projection okay.
```
def coord_search(h, deadline, rng, alphas=None, topk=120, line_evals=32, max_no_improve=4):
    n=len(h)
    dx=2.0/n
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy()
    best_c=compute_c5(best_h,n)
    if alphas is None:
       if n <= 200: alphas=(100.0, 800.0, 4000.0)
       elif n <= 400: alphas=(100.0, 800.0)
       else: alphas=(80.0, 500.0)
    if n > 500:
       topk = max(40, min(topk, n//5))
       line_evals = max(22, min(line_evals, 28))
    for alpha in alphas:
       no_imp=0
       while time.time() < deadline - 1.0 and no_imp < max_no_improve:
          # project check
          if h.max() > 1.0 + 1e-9 or h.min() < -1e-9 or abs(float(h.sum()) - n*0.5) > 1e-9:
              h = project_feasible(h,n)
          C = compute_C_raw(h,n)
          M = float(C.max())
          if M*(2.0/n) < best_c - 1e-14:
              best_c = M*(2.0/n); best_h = h.copy()
          w = weights_alpha(C, 2.0/n, alpha)
          g = weighted_gradient(h,w,n)
          cand = make_candidates(h,g,n,rng,topk)
          accepted=0
          for item in cand:
             if time.time() > deadline - 0.5: break
             _, i, j = item
             lo = max(-h[i], h[j] - 1.0)
             hi = min(1.0 - h[i], h[j])
             if hi <= lo + 1e-14: continue
             lo += 1e-12
             hi -= 1e-12
             if hi <= lo: continue
             V = pair_update_vector(h,i,j,n)
             d = abs(i-j)
             r1 = n - 1 - d
             r2 = n - 1 + d
             res = pair_line_search(C,V,r1,r2,lo,hi,M,line_evals,max(1e-12, 1e-10*M))
             if res is not None:
                delta,newM = res
                delta = min(max(delta, lo), hi)
                if abs(delta) < 1e-16: continue
                q=delta*delta
                C += delta*V
                C[r1] += q
                if r2 != r1: C[r2] += q
                h[i] += delta; h[j] -= delta
                M = newM
                accepted += 1
                if M*(2.0/n) < best_c:
                    best_c = M*(2.0/n); best_h=h.copy()
          if accepted == 0:
              no_imp += 1
          else:
              no_imp = 0
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h,n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h,n)
    return best_h, float(best_c)
```
Using `h.max()` creates scalar. If h length small. Good.
After phase ends, best_h projected maybe c increases. But accept only if projected c lower? The `best_c` before projection might be slightly lower. At final we project and recompute, so returned c feasible. Good.

One subtlety: During sweep, `C` is updated but after each sweep we recompute from h. At start of phase, h projected. After many accepts, h may drift sum. Project check at start next sweep. Good.
Best_h copy at each accept before final projection. If final projection increases, best_c returned might be higher than a non-projected accept c. But feasible required. Fine.

`block_objective`, `block_sum_fun`, `block_sum_jac`.
```
def block_objective(x, h_full, idx, n, alpha):
    h = h_full.copy()
    h[idx] = x
    val, g = smooth_obj_jac(h, n, alpha)
    return val, g[idx]
```
`idx` could be list/array. `g[idx]` returns array.
`block_sum_jac(x,target): return np.ones(len(x))` (not ones_like to avoid if x maybe not? both fine).

`block_refine`:
```
def block_refine(h,n,deadline,rng,alpha=1000.0,block_size=24,maxiter=100):
   best_h=project_feasible(h,n); best_c=compute_c5(best_h,n)
   no_imp=0
   target_total=n*0.5
   while time.time() < deadline - 4.0 and no_imp < 3:
      C=compute_C_raw(best_h,n); dx=2.0/n
      w=weights_alpha(C,dx,alpha)
      g=weighted_gradient(best_h,w,n)
      order=np.argsort(g)
      half=block_size//2
      low=order[:half]
      high=order[-(block_size-half):][::-1]
      idx_list = np.concatenate((low, high))
      # random merge 30%
      if rng is not None and np.random? Use rng.rand()
```
No random global. Use `if rng is not None and rng.rand() < 0.35:`. `rng` RandomState.
```
          rand_idx = rng.choice(n, max(2, block_size//2), replace=False)
          idx_list = np.concatenate((idx_list, rand_idx))
      idx = np.unique(idx_list)
      if idx.size > block_size:
          # keep by average? np.unique sorted; maybe just first block_size
          idx = idx[:block_size]
      if idx.size < 2:
          break
      fixed_sum = float(best_h.sum()) - float(best_h[idx].sum())
      target = target_total - fixed_sum
      if target < -1e-10 or target > idx.size*(1.0+1e-10):
          no_imp +=1; continue
      x0 = best_h[idx].copy()
      bounds = [(0.0,1.0)] * int(idx.size)
      cons = [{'type':'eq','fun':block_sum_fun,'jac':block_sum_jac,'args':(float(target),)}]
      try:
         res = minimize(block_objective, x0, args=(best_h, idx, n, float(alpha)), method='SLSQP', jac=True, bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'ftol':1e-12})
         h_new = best_h.copy()
         h_new[idx] = res.x
         h_new = project_feasible(h_new,n)
         c_new = compute_c5(h_new,n)
         if c_new < best_c - 1e-13:
             best_h = h_new; best_c = float(c_new); no_imp=0
         else:
             no_imp += 1
      except Exception:
         no_imp += 1
   return best_h, float(best_c)
```

def optimize_grid(h,n,deadline,rng,do_slsqp=True):
    h = project_feasible(h,n)

           t1 = time.time() + min(140.0, rem*0.35)

    # block refine if time

`run`: Use time and global deadline. Need if budget_s less? default 1100.
```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    if budget_s is None: budget_s=1100
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 4.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))
    init = globals().get('initial_h_values', None)
    if init is not None:
       try:
          h0 = np.asarray(init, dtype=float).copy()
          n0 = int(len(h0))
          if n0 < 21:
             n0=250; h0=default_start(250)
       except Exception:
          h0=default_start(250); n0=250
    else:
       h0=default_start(250); n0=250
    best_h=project_feasible(h0,n0); best_c=compute_c5(best_h,n0); best_n=n0
    # quick transform sym maybe
    hs=0.5*(best_h+best_h[::-1]); hs=project_feasible(hs,n0); cs=compute_c5(hs,n0); if cs<best_c update
    # main
    rem = deadline - time.time()
    main_alloc = min(520.0, max(60.0, rem * 0.55))
    dl1 = t0 + main_alloc
    if dl1 > deadline - 40: dl1 = deadline - 40
    if dl1 > t0 + 10:
       h2,c2=optimize_grid(best_h,best_n,dl1,rng,do_slsqp=True)
       if c2<best_c: update
    # other grids
    # candidates based on best_n current
    tried=set([best_n])
    order_list = [best_n-5,best_n+5,best_n-10,best_n+10,best_n-20,best_n+20,best_n-30,best_n+30,best_n-50,best_n+50,100,150,200,300,400,500]
    candidates=[]
    for nn in order_list:
       nn=int(nn)
       if 60 <= nn <= 700 and nn not in tried:
          tried.add(nn); candidates.append(nn)
    noimp=0; trials=0
    for nn in candidates:
       if time.time() > deadline - 70: break
       if trials >= 6: break
       if nn == best_n: continue
       rem = deadline - time.time() - 20
       if rem < 35: break
       alloc = min(75.0, max(30.0, rem * 0.15))
       if nn > 500: alloc = max(30.0, alloc * 0.6)
       trial_dl = time.time() + alloc
       if trial_dl > deadline - 25: trial_dl = deadline - 25
       if trial_dl <= time.time(): continue
       h_interp = interpolate_to_n(best_h,best_n,nn)
       do = (alloc > 50 and nn <= 350)
       h2,c2=optimize_grid(h_interp,nn,trial_dl,rng,do_slsqp=do)
       if c2 < best_c:
          best_c=c2; best_h=h2; best_n=nn; noimp=0
       else:
          noimp += 1
       trials += 1
       if noimp >= 3 and trials >= 3: break
    # restarts
    scales=[0.015,0.03,0.06,0.12]
    r=0
    while r < 4 and time.time() < deadline - 45:
       scale=scales[r % len(scales)]
       hp=best_h + rng.normal(0.0, scale, best_n)
       hp=project_feasible(hp,best_n)
       rem = deadline - time.time() - 15
       if rem < 25: break
       alloc = min(65.0, max(20.0, rem * 0.35))
       trial_dl = time.time() + alloc
       if trial_dl > deadline - 10: trial_dl = deadline - 10
       if trial_dl <= time.time(): break
       do = (best_n <= 300 and rem > 90 and r == 0)
       h2,c2=optimize_grid(hp,best_n,trial_dl,rng,do_slsqp=do)
       if c2 < best_c:
          best_c=c2; best_h=h2
       r += 1
    # final polish
    if time.time() < deadline - 8:
       h2,c2=optimize_grid(best_h,best_n,deadline-3.0,rng,do_slsqp=False)
       if c2 < best_c: best_c=c2; best_h=h2
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,best_n)
    best_c=compute_c5(best_h,best_n)
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```

Source contribution idx2 from p=1, q=1: y? source slice i=1 indices1..3 values y2,y1,y0. idx2 gets y1. Δ1 idx2 = y1 - h1. Good! I mistakenly paired source idx2 y1 with -h0. Correct. ```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


_GOLDEN_FRAC = 0.3819660112501051


def project_feasible(v, n):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float).copy()
    if n <= 0:
        return v
    target = float(n) * 0.5
    if target <= 1e-15:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if target >= float(n) - 1e-15:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=float)

    vmin = float(np.min(v))
    vmax = float(np.max(v))
    lo = vmin - 1.0
    hi = vmax + 1.0

    for _ in range(70):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        p = np.clip(v - mid, 0.0, 1.0)
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid

    p = np.clip(v - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)

    for _ in range(6):
        diff = target - float(p.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = np.where(p < 1.0)[0]
            if idx.size == 0:
                break
            step = diff / float(idx.size)
            for k in idx:
                add = min(step, 1.0 - p[k])
                p[k] += add
                diff -= add
                if diff <= 1e-15:
                    break
        else:
            idx = np.where(p > 0.0)[0]
            if idx.size == 0:
                break
            step = -diff / float(idx.size)
            for k in idx:
                sub = min(step, p[k])
                p[k] -= sub
                diff += sub
                if diff >= -1e-15:
                    break
    return p


def compute_C_raw(h, n):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full').astype(float, copy=False)


def compute_c5(h, n):
    C = compute_C_raw(h, n)
    return float(C.max()) * (2.0 / float(n))


def weights_alpha(C, dx, alpha):
    alpha = float(alpha)
    if alpha <= 0.0:
        m = int(C.argmax())
        w = np.zeros_like(C, dtype=float)
        w[m] = 1.0
        return w
    mraw = float(C.max())
    shifted = alpha * float(dx) * (C - mraw)
    e = np.exp(np.clip(shifted, -740.0, 0.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w = np.zeros_like(C, dtype=float)
        w[int(C.argmax())] = 1.0
        return w
    return e / s


def weighted_gradient(h, w, n):
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    w0 = float(w[n - 1])
    if w0 != 0.0:
        g += w0 * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)

    for d in range(1, n):
        w_low = float(w[n - 1 - d])
        w_high = float(w[n - 1 + d])
        a = slice(0, n - d)
        b = slice(d, n)

        if w_low != 0.0:
            g[a] += w_low * (1.0 - h[b])
            g[b] -= w_low * h[a]

        if w_high != 0.0:
            g[b] += w_high * (1.0 - h[a])
            g[a] -= w_high * h[b]

    return g


def smooth_obj_jac(x, n, alpha):
    x = np.clip(np.asarray(x, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    C = compute_C_raw(x, n)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    mraw = float(C.max())
    alpha = float(alpha)

    if alpha <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(C, dtype=float)
        w[int(C.argmax())] = 1.0
        return mraw * dx, dx * weighted_gradient(x, w, n)

    shifted = alpha * dx * (C - mraw)
    e = np.exp(np.clip(shifted, -740.0, 0.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w = np.zeros_like(C, dtype=float)
        w[int(C.argmax())] = 1.0
        s = 1.0
    else:
        w = e / s

    value = mraw * dx + float(np.log(s)) / alpha
    grad = dx * weighted_gradient(x, w, n)
    return float(value), grad


def sum_equal_fun(x, n):
    return float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * float(n))


def sum_equal_jac(x, n):
    return np.ones(len(x), dtype=float)


def slsqp_smooth(h, n, alpha, maxiter):
    if maxiter <= 0:
        return project_feasible(h, n)

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * int(n)
    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': sum_equal_fun, 'jac': sum_equal_jac, 'args': (int(n),)}]
    try:
        res = minimize(
            smooth_obj_jac,
            h,
            args=(int(n), float(alpha)),
            method='SLSQP',
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'ftol': 1e-12}
        )
        return project_feasible(res.x, n)
    except Exception:
        return project_feasible(h, n)


def eval_pair_delta(C, V, r1, r2, delta):
    if delta == 0.0:
        return float(C.max())
    base = C + delta * V
    best = float(base.max())
    q = delta * delta
    v1 = base[r1] + q
    if v1 > best:
        best = float(v1)
    if r2 != r1:
        v2 = base[r2] + q
        if v2 > best:
            best = float(v2)
    return best


def pair_line_search(C, V, r1, r2, lo, hi, current_M, maxit=32, tol=1e-10):
    lo = float(lo)
    hi = float(hi)
    span = hi - lo
    if span <= 1e-14:
        return None

    best_d = 0.0
    best_y = float(current_M)

    c1 = lo + _GOLDEN_FRAC * span
    c2 = hi - _GOLDEN_FRAC * span

    y1 = eval_pair_delta(C, V, r1, r2, c1)
    if y1 < best_y - tol:
        best_y = y1
        best_d = c1

    y2 = eval_pair_delta(C, V, r1, r2, c2)
    if y2 < best_y - tol:
        best_y = y2
        best_d = c2

    for _ in range(int(maxit)):
        if hi - lo <= 1e-13 * max(1.0, abs(lo), abs(hi)):
            break

        if y1 < y2:
            hi = c2
            c2 = c1
            y2 = y1
            c1 = lo + _GOLDEN_FRAC * (hi - lo)
            y1 = eval_pair_delta(C, V, r1, r2, c1)
            if y1 < best_y - tol:
                best_y = y1
                best_d = c1
        else:
            lo = c1
            c1 = c2
            y1 = y2
            c2 = hi - _GOLDEN_FRAC * (hi - lo)
            y2 = eval_pair_delta(C, V, r1, r2, c2)
            if y2 < best_y - tol:
                best_y = y2
                best_d = c2

    for dd in (lo, hi):
        y = eval_pair_delta(C, V, r1, r2, dd)
        if y < best_y - tol:
            best_y = y
            best_d = dd

    if best_y < float(current_M) - tol:
        return (float(best_d), float(best_y))
    return None


def pair_update_vector(h, i, j, n):
    m = 2 * n - 1
    V = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
    yrev = (1.0 - h)[::-1]
    neg_h = -h

    V[i : n + i] += yrev
    V[n - 1 - i : 2 * n - 1 - i] += neg_h

    V[j : n + j] -= yrev
    V[n - 1 - j : 2 * n - 1 - j] += h

    return V


def make_candidates(h, g, n, rng, topk):
    cand = []
    seen = set()
    L = max(4, min(30, n // 8))
    order = np.argsort(g)
    low = order[:L]
    high = order[-L:][::-1]

    for ii in low:
        i = int(ii)
        for jj in high:
            j = int(jj)
            if i == j:
                continue
            if float(g[i]) < float(g[j]):
                i2, j2 = i, j
                score = float(g[j] - g[i])
            else:
                i2, j2 = j, i
                score = float(g[i] - g[j])
            if score <= 1e-10:
                continue
            hi = min(1.0 - h[i2], h[j2])
            if hi <= 1e-14:
                continue
            key = min(i2, j2) * int(n) + max(i2, j2)
            if key not in seen:
                seen.add(key)
                cand.append((score, i2, j2))

    nrand = min(220, max(int(topk), 60))
    for _ in range(nrand):
        i = int(rng.randint(n))
        j = int(rng.randint(n))
        if i == j:
            continue
        if float(g[i]) < float(g[j]):
            i2, j2 = i, j
            score = float(g[j] - g[i])
        else:
            i2, j2 = j, i
            score = float(g[i] - g[j])
        if score <= 1e-10:
            continue
        hi = min(1.0 - h[i2], h[j2])
        if hi <= 1e-14:
            continue
        key = min(i2, j2) * int(n) + max(i2, j2)
        if key not in seen:
            seen.add(key)
            cand.append((score, i2, j2))

    cand.sort(reverse=True)
    if len(cand) > int(topk):
        cand = cand[:int(topk)]
    return cand


def coord_search(h, deadline, rng, alphas=None, topk=120, line_evals=32, max_no_improve=4):
    n = int(len(h))
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n)

    if alphas is None:
        if n <= 200:
            alphas = (100.0, 800.0, 4000.0)
        elif n <= 400:
            alphas = (120.0, 900.0)
        else:
            alphas = (80.0, 500.0)

    if n > 500:
        topk = max(40, min(int(topk), n // 5))
        line_evals = max(22, min(int(line_evals), 28))

    dx = 2.0 / float(n)

    for alpha in alphas:
        no_imp = 0
        while time.time() < float(deadline) - 1.0 and no_imp < int(max_no_improve):
            if (h.max() > 1.0 + 1e-9 or h.min() < -1e-9 or
                    abs(float(h.sum()) - 0.5 * n) > 1e-9):
                h = project_feasible(h, n)

            C = compute_C_raw(h, n)
            M = float(C.max())
            obj = M * dx

            if obj < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c = float(obj)
                best_h = h.copy()

            w = weights_alpha(C, dx, alpha)
            g = weighted_gradient(h, w, n)
            cand = make_candidates(h, g, n, rng, topk)

            accepted = 0
            for item in cand:
                if time.time() > float(deadline) - 0.5:
                    break
                _, i, j = item

                lo = max(-h[i], h[j] - 1.0)
                hi = min(1.0 - h[i], h[j])
                if hi <= lo + 1e-14:
                    continue

                lo += 1e-12
                hi -= 1e-12
                if hi <= lo:
                    continue

                V = pair_update_vector(h, i, j, n)
                d = abs(int(i) - int(j))
                r1 = n - 1 - d
                r2 = n - 1 + d

                res = pair_line_search(C, V, r1, r2, lo, hi, M,
                                       int(line_evals), max(1e-12, 1e-10 * M))
                if res is None:
                    continue

                delta, newM = res
                delta = min(max(float(delta), float(lo)), float(hi))
                if abs(delta) < 1e-16:
                    continue

                q = delta * delta
                C += delta * V
                C[r1] += q
                if r2 != r1:
                    C[r2] += q

                h[i] += delta
                h[j] -= delta
                M = float(newM)
                accepted += 1

                obj = M * dx
                if obj < best_c:
                    best_c = float(obj)
                    best_h = h.copy()

            if accepted == 0:
                no_imp += 1
            else:
                no_imp = 0

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n)
    return best_h, float(best_c)


def block_objective(x, h_full, idx, n, alpha):
    h = h_full.copy()
    h[idx] = x
    val, g = smooth_obj_jac(h, n, alpha)
    return val, g[idx]


def block_sum_fun(x, target):
    return float(np.sum(x) - float(target))


def block_sum_jac(x, target):
    return np.ones(len(x), dtype=float)


def block_refine(h, n, deadline, rng, alpha=1000.0, block_size=24, maxiter=100):
    best_h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n)
    target_total = 0.5 * float(n)
    no_imp = 0

    while time.time() < float(deadline) - 4.0 and no_imp < 3:
        C = compute_C_raw(best_h, n)
        dx = 2.0 / float(n)
        w = weights_alpha(C, dx, alpha)
        g = weighted_gradient(best_h, w, n)
        order = np.argsort(g)

        half = max(2, int(block_size) // 2)
        low = order[:half]
        high = order[-(int(block_size) - half):][::-1]
        idx_list = np.concatenate((low, high))

        if rng is not None and rng.rand() < 0.35:
            rand_idx = rng.choice(n, max(2, int(block_size) // 2), replace=False)
            idx_list = np.concatenate((idx_list, rand_idx))

        idx = np.unique(idx_list)
        if idx.size > int(block_size):
            idx = idx[:int(block_size)]
        if idx.size < 2:
            break

        fixed_sum = float(best_h.sum()) - float(best_h[idx].sum())
        target = target_total - fixed_sum
        if target < -1e-10 or target > float(idx.size) * (1.0 + 1e-10):
            no_imp += 1
            continue

        x0 = best_h[idx].copy()
        bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * int(idx.size)
        cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': block_sum_fun, 'jac': block_sum_jac,
                 'args': (float(target),)}]

        try:
            res = minimize(
                block_objective,
                x0,
                args=(best_h, idx, int(n), float(alpha)),
                method='SLSQP',
                jac=True,
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=cons,
                options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'ftol': 1e-12}
            )
            h_new = best_h.copy()
            h_new[idx] = res.x
            h_new = project_feasible(h_new, n)
            c_new = compute_c5(h_new, n)
            if c_new < best_c - 1e-13:
                best_h = h_new
                best_c = float(c_new)
                no_imp = 0
            else:
                no_imp += 1
        except Exception:
            no_imp += 1

    return best_h, float(best_c)


def optimize_grid(h, n, deadline, rng, do_slsqp=True):
    n = int(n)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n)

    rem = float(deadline) - time.time()
    if rem > 15.0:
        if rem > 180.0:
            t1 = time.time() + min(140.0, rem * 0.35)
        else:
            t1 = time.time() + rem * 0.55
        t1 = min(t1, float(deadline) - 5.0)
        if t1 > time.time():
            h2, c2 = coord_search(h, t1, rng)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_c = c2
                best_h = h2

    if time.time() < float(deadline) - 8.0:
        hs = 0.5 * (best_h + best_h[::-1])
        hs = project_feasible(hs, n)
        cs = compute_c5(hs, n)
        if cs < best_c:
            best_c = cs
            best_h = hs

    if n <= 600:
        rem_block = float(deadline) - time.time()
        if rem_block > 25.0:
            bd = time.time() + min(60.0, rem_block * 0.25)
            h2, c2 = block_refine(best_h, n, bd, rng, alpha=600.0, block_size=24, maxiter=80)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_c = c2
                best_h = h2

    if n <= 400:
        rem_block = float(deadline) - time.time()
        if rem_block > 25.0:
            bd = time.time() + min(60.0, rem_block * 0.25)
            h2, c2 = block_refine(best_h, n, bd, rng, alpha=3000.0, block_size=18, maxiter=70)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_c = c2
                best_h = h2

    if do_slsqp and n <= 400 and time.time() < float(deadline) - 30.0:
        alphas = [1200.0, 5000.0]
        if n <= 250 and time.time() < float(deadline) - 60.0:
            alphas.append(20000.0)

        for alpha in alphas:
            if time.time() > float(deadline) - 15.0:
                break
            rem = float(deadline) - time.time()
            if rem < 12.0:
                break
            if n <= 150:
                mi = 80
            elif n <= 300:
                mi = 50
            else:
                mi = 30

            ht = slsqp_smooth(best_h, n, alpha, mi)
            ct = compute_c5(ht, n)
            if ct < best_c:
                best_c = ct
                best_h = ht

    if time.time() < float(deadline) - 6.0:
        if n <= 300:
            final_alphas = (1500.0, 8000.0)
        else:
            final_alphas = (700.0,)
        h2, c2 = coord_search(best_h, float(deadline) - 3.0, rng,
                              alphas=final_alphas, topk=90, line_evals=30, max_no_improve=3)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h2

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n)
    return best_h, float(best_c)


def interpolate_to_n(h_old, n_old, n_new):
    n_old = int(n_old)
    n_new = int(n_new)
    if n_old == n_new:
        return project_feasible(np.asarray(h_old, dtype=float).copy(), n_new)

    dx_old = 2.0 / float(n_old)
    dx_new = 2.0 / float(n_new)
    x_old = (np.arange(n_old, dtype=float) + 0.5) * dx_old
    x_new = (np.arange(n_new, dtype=float) + 0.5) * dx_new
    h_new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, np.asarray(h_old, dtype=float),
                      left=float(h_old[0]), right=float(h_old[-1]))
    return project_feasible(h_new, n_new)


def default_start(n):
    n = int(n)
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    h = 0.5 + 0.38 * np.cos(np.pi * x) - 0.08 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    try:
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 1100.0
    deadline = t0 + budget_s - 4.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))

    init = globals().get('initial_h_values', None)
    if init is not None:
        try:
            h0 = np.asarray(init, dtype=float).copy()
            n0 = int(len(h0))
            if n0 < 20:
                h0 = default_start(250)
                n0 = 250
        except Exception:
            h0 = default_start(250)
            n0 = 250
    else:
        h0 = default_start(250)
        n0 = 250

    best_h = project_feasible(h0, n0)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n0)
    best_n = n0

    hs = 0.5 * (best_h + best_h[::-1])
    hs = project_feasible(hs, best_n)
    cs = compute_c5(hs, best_n)
    if cs < best_c:
        best_c = cs
        best_h = hs

    rem = deadline - time.time()
    main_alloc = min(520.0, max(60.0, rem * 0.55))
    dl1 = t0 + main_alloc
    dl1 = min(dl1, deadline - 40.0)
    if dl1 > t0 + 10.0:
        h2, c2 = optimize_grid(best_h, best_n, dl1, rng, do_slsqp=True)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h2

    tried = set([best_n])
    order_list = [
        best_n - 5, best_n + 5, best_n - 10, best_n + 10,
        best_n - 20, best_n + 20, best_n - 30, best_n + 30,
        best_n - 50, best_n + 50, 100, 150, 200, 300, 400, 500
    ]
    candidates = []
    for nn in order_list:
        nn = int(nn)
        if 60 <= nn <= 700 and nn not in tried:
            tried.add(nn)
            candidates.append(nn)

    noimp = 0
    trials = 0
    for nn in candidates:
        if time.time() > deadline - 70.0:
            break
        if trials >= 6:
            break
        if nn == best_n:
            continue

        rem = deadline - time.time() - 20.0
        if rem < 35.0:
            break
        alloc = min(75.0, max(30.0, rem * 0.15))
        if nn > 500:
            alloc = max(30.0, alloc * 0.6)

        trial_dl = time.time() + alloc
        trial_dl = min(trial_dl, deadline - 25.0)
        if trial_dl <= time.time():
            continue

        h_interp = interpolate_to_n(best_h, best_n, nn)
        do = (alloc > 50.0 and nn <= 350)
        h2, c2 = optimize_grid(h_interp, nn, trial_dl, rng, do_slsqp=do)

        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h2
            best_n = nn
            noimp = 0
        else:
            noimp += 1

        trials += 1
        if noimp >= 3 and trials >= 3:
            break

    scales = [0.015, 0.03, 0.06, 0.12]
    r = 0
    while r < 4 and time.time() < deadline - 45.0:
        scale = scales[r % len(scales)]
        hp = best_h + rng.normal(0.0, float(scale), best_n)
        hp = project_feasible(hp, best_n)

        rem = deadline - time.time() - 15.0
        if rem < 25.0:
            break
        alloc = min(65.0, max(20.0, rem * 0.35))
        trial_dl = time.time() + alloc
        trial_dl = min(trial_dl, deadline - 10.0)
        if trial_dl <= time.time():
            break

        do = (best_n <= 300 and rem > 90.0 and r == 0)
        h2, c2 = optimize_grid(hp, best_n, trial_dl, rng, do_slsqp=do)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h2

        r += 1

    if time.time() < deadline - 8.0:
        h2, c2 = optimize_grid(best_h, best_n, deadline - 3.0, rng, do_slsqp=False)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h2

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
